The next-packet helper later corrected the live record drift by keeping private/home on `PDV.Signal.DunmerHome.Activator`, moving portable to `PDV.Signal.DunmerPortableShrine.Activator`, and adding strict verifier coverage for distinct Phase 10 cooldown keys.
- **Phase 10/11 doc-grilled handoff (2026-05-24):** Phase 10/11 are subsystem labels, but `PDV_Architecture_v3.md` Section 21.5 remains the execution-order authority. Phase 10 proceeds as Dunmer ancestor substrate proof graduation, reusing the already proven portable/private shrine ACTI surfaces and requiring evidence that substrate progress stays separate from patron piety. Phase 11 D-10 is resolved to an Arngeir/Kynareth recognition pilot gated by Nord origin, Kyne active deity, Champion tier, and Arngeir as speaker; live dialogue implementation is prep-only again after the generated-record CTD remediation. Rationale: this preserves the reduced Pattern Proving order while letting verifier/docs prep remove ambiguity before the privilege work starts.
- **CKRA GLOB duplicate-create proof and CK UX lesson (2026-05-23):** `C:\Users\Admin\Documents\ckra-native` now has one proof-ledger-supported CKPE authoring surface: `glob.duplicate_create` for disposable generated proof plugins. The proven product path duplicates `PDV_GLO_ActivePiety` through CK's Object Window duplicate command, mutates the duplicate GLOB FNAM/FLTV payload from float `0` to short `1`, posts CK's native save command, reads the saved ESP back directly, and promotes only GLOB duplicate-create in the generated capability matrix. Durable user-experience lesson: CK's visible state is load-bearing. Plain `WM_COMMAND` replay failed until the automation matched the human right-click context-menu path closely enough: active plugin, Object Window list focus, selected source row matching the explicit source, popup observation, and main-thread pumping. Durable lower-layer lesson: in-memory mutation is not product proof until CK save and direct filesystem readback prove the active plugin contains the intended record. This does not close any PDV gameplay phase and does not prove generic `createRecord`, quest/message/activator/FormList creation, VMAD array writes, Story Manager authoring, alias creation, or source-plugin promotion.
- **Phase 8 runtime proof closeout and overlay lesson (2026-05-21):** Phase 8 is now fully runtime-proven on an Imperial save. The ConcordatStanding track passed baseline raw-value movement, pending transition persistence/cancel, 3-day lock-in commit, committed-state multiplier readback, extreme-band inward-halving while locked, save/load persistence, gate unlock, and 3-day exit back to `PublicCompliant`. Durable implementation lesson: the generated `PDV_Phase8ConcordatTalosOverlay.esp` was not a safe steady-state runtime patch because a partial VMAD override could win with blank/default Talos script values (`DeityName` empty, `DeityIndex = 0`). The live fix is manager-owned runtime wiring plus save-healing in `PDV__ManagerQuest` (`EnsurePhase8RuntimeWiring()` and `EnsureTalosRuntimeIdentity()`), while the strict Phase 8 closeout gate stays clean at `FAIL=0, WARN=0, TODO=0, PASS=642, INFO=28`.
- **Phase 9 Bosmer source/tooling/runtime closeout (2026-05-21 through 2026-05-24; proof placements retired 2026-06-16):** The shared `PDV_StateTrack` transition contract is now live in source with offered state, pending state, three evidence-day windows, refusal/lockout handling, and explicit confirm/cancel APIs. Bosmer is the first consumer: `PDV__ManagerQuest` now owns a post-startup Bosmer path chooser, Bosmer-specific dawn suggestion evaluation, PactBound/GreenPactCompliance/LapsedFromPact handling, path-specific foreground patron switching, and a shared confirmation-rite route through EventBus/receiver scripts. `PDV_Deity_Yffre`, `PDV_Deity_Zen`, and `PDV_Deity_BaanDar` compile and have live framework record/property readback for path eligibility, while `tools/pdv_verify.mjs --strict-phase9` gates the Bosmer state-track contract, manager wiring, deity eligibility properties, messages, and the retired Windhelm proof-surface editor IDs. The five former manual proof REFRs (`PDV_REFR_BosmerLivingStorySignal`, `PDV_REFR_BosmerExchangeSignal`, `PDV_REFR_BosmerBanditRoadSignal`, `PDV_REFR_BosmerPactPositiveSignal`, and `PDV_REFR_StateTransitionConfirmRite`) and their `PDV_ACTI_*` bases were removed on 2026-06-16 because they remained visible in `WindhelmTempleofTalos`; `tools/pdv-phase9-author -- --check-placements` now fails if they return. Counted runtime proof passed historically for all five proof-surface routes, Living Story/Exchange/Bandit Road/Old Contract offers, confirmation-rite switching, Old Contract re-entry, PactBound/compliance separation, forced reckoning `Renounce`, forced reckoning `Recommit`, and save/load persistence. Runtime proof surfaced and fixed a real state-track bug: Old Contract re-entry required three Pact-positive evidence days, but the original tracker only retained two; `PDV_StateTrack` now retains `LatestDay`, `PreviousDay`, and `ThirdDay`. Compile lesson: from sandboxed agent sessions, PapyrusCompiler may fail before syntax analysis with `Access to the path ... .pas is denied`; rerun outside the sandbox to let it write temporary compiler files in the MO2 mod folder.

- **Daedric decisions D-15..D-18 locked (2026-05-31 AEST):** Closed the open Daedric content decisions so CAT-4 (the 15 non-pilot Princes, the Phase 20C long pole) can begin without re-author risk. `PDV_Architecture_v3.md` Section 11.6 now locks **D-15** (four-band `WitchcraftExposure` stigma model: per-Prince `PDV.Daedric.<Prince>.Stigma`, derived `PDV_GLO_DaedricExposure = max(active path stigma)` social read, `Tolerated`/`Standard`/`High-rupture` weight classes, 1/day decay, `WasChampion` residue flag), **D-16** (curse-access template for Hircine/Molag Bal: curse-onset replaces the pact, curse-state-driven stigma, cure-path exit, no-double-fire with race-manifest `CurseState` rows; Hircine is content-surface-only since its Phase 13/15 mechanics are proven), **D-17** (template-variation proof batch - Azura, Vaermina, Meridia, Molag Bal - before volume), and **D-18** (per-Prince content-ready checklist for the 20C gate). The prior open list's roster/cross-Prince/recovery items were already closed by D-12/13/14. Rationale of record: `references/authoring/PDV_Daedric_DecisionPacket_CAT4.md`. Manifest Section 5 flipped provisional->locked; Section 7 records the curse-access/order locks; coverage matrix 20C now carries a `contentReadyDefinition` (D-18) and `voicedContentScope` field. Locked into main on reconcile at architecture v3.77 (combined with the voice deferral). Design lock only - no source/record/verifier behavior change; content verifier clean (exit 0).

On reconciling the amazing-goodall branch into main it was found redundant with main's parallel `references/authoring/PDV_PreBetaRaceAcceptanceRubric.md` (plus `PDV_PreBetaRaceScalingSpine.md` and `PDV_PreBetaRaceGateLedger.md`) and was dropped; the QASmoke runbook now points at main's rubric chain, which is the single acceptance bar.

On reconcile it was moved out of `PDV_RaceRewardBudgetLedger.md` (now a pointer) into the per-race "Completed Race Rows" of main's `references/authoring/PDV_RaceEffectReviewLedger.md`, the canonical effect-review gate whose "Completion Bar Per Race" names the same ten fields. The broad/floor-ceiling layer of that gate is now answered for all ten races; magnitudes stay provisional and deeper per-effect review remains on the table's full-review tracker.
- **Voiced NPC dialogue deferred to V2 (2026-05-31 AEST):** Owner decision - V1 (1.0) ships no voiced NPC dialogue because credible spoken lines need voice files and voice work is held for V2. `PDV_Architecture_v3.md` Section 21.3 gains a "no voiced content in 1.0" non-goal plus a scope-mapping rule (any "dialogue privilege"/"recognition" payoff is delivered via non-voiced surfaces in V1; the spoken version is V2). Deferred to V2: the Phase 11 privilege dialogue/restoration/recognition families and the Arngeir/Kynareth pilot (Sections 9, 9.4); the live Phase 18 Nord recognition quartet (Froki, Heimskr, Andurs, Aela); the 39 `PDV_Dlog_*_Recognition` stubs (CAT-1 drafted, excluded from CAT-6 V1 promotion). Staying in V1: Shrine privilege, mechanical Recognition (faction stance), non-dialogue Threshold privilege, all MessageBox/notification/Survey text. Content-Feel Beta (25.6) and 1.0 (25.7) gates no longer require dialogue/recognition texture. `PDV_TargetEndStates_1.0.md` gains a Voiced Content Scope section; Nord dialogue boundary marked V2. New `references/authoring/PDV_V2_Backlog.md` holds the deferred work. Locked into main on reconcile at architecture v3.77; main's `PDV_RecognitionDialogueScalePacket.md` and the dialogue half of `PDV_CAT6PromotionPilot.md` are V2-flagged, not deleted. **Pending CK-side build action (cannot be done from docs):** disable/remove the live Phase 18 Nord dialogue `DLBR`/`DIAL`/`INFO` records from the V1 release ESP; move their Phase 18 dialogue verifier assertions to V2 scope. This retires the CK-safe dialogue authoring pattern as a V1 risk.

- **Broad reward authoring gate resolved (2026-05-31 AEST):** `references/authoring/PDV_RaceRewardBudgetLedger.md` gains a "Broad Reward Spec (per race)" section that unblocks broad-reward authoring. Every race now names the ten required fields - floor effect family, ceiling effect family, magnitude range, cadence, grant/removal owner, stack cap, Survey/status copy, rejected generic hooks, curse/Daedric interaction, and manual feel note. Grounded in the locked per-race focused passes and the architecture broad-lane rule: real broad-worship specs for Nord (Old Ways/Nine Divines), Imperial (Nine Divines), and Redguard (sect-shaped); layered/substrate baselines for Dunmer, Khajiit, and Argonian; and explicit "no generic broad lane" baseline-equivalent rows for Bosmer (path), Orc (life mode), Breton (tradition), and Altmer (Auri-El coherence). A shared magnitude convention sets broad floor minimal and broad ceiling at Faithful, roughly 50-70% of the focused-patron Devoted value, with all numbers flagged pre-tuning. Doc-only authoring gate; no source/record change.
- **Content-authoring ratification audit + stale Daedric-gate fix (2026-05-31 AEST; CAT-6 status superseded 2026-06-07):** A whole-roster gap audit confirmed the V1 manifest prose is content-complete: `pdv_content_verify` clean (FAIL=0, WARN=0, 1,065 rows), all 10 races and all 16 Skyrim-present Princes carry their full V1 surface set, no required V1 surface missing, and guardrail spot-checks passed. The 1.0 Acceptance Tracker "Content authored" column is flipped from `Drafted` to `Ratified` for all 10 races (new status value defined), and coverage matrix 20B/20C carry `contentAuthoringStatus` notes. At that point, remaining content work was CAT-6 ESP promotion (code track) and V2 voiced dialogue; as of 2026-06-07, all-Prince CAT-6 record/readback is complete and the remaining Daedric blockers are runtime/display proof, live sender proof, wrong/generic-source silence, stack/Survey legibility, and deeper curse-access checks. **Reconciliation of a Codex "all missing" report:** the strict Phase 20 verifier read `references/authoring/PDV_Phase20_NoInGameProof_Gates.json` `daedricBlockers`, which still marked stigma/curse-access/Prince-order as `open` (stale - D-15/D-16/D-17 resolved them this session) and `tools/pdv_verify.mjs` line ~3734 asserted they must be `open` to pass, so it reported Daedric as blocked. Fixed both: the gate JSON now marks the three contracts `resolved-by-D15/D16/D17` (keeping `runtimePromotionAllowed: false`, since promotion is still gated), and the verifier now passes when decisions are resolved and promotion stays gated.

- **Curse-access stigma = Model B; notoriety enhancement deferred to V2 (2026-06-01):** During the Daedric prose review, the Hircine/Molag Bal curse-access Princes were found carrying full independent per-act stigma crossing rows, which violated D-16 (curse-access stigma must be curse-state-driven). Chose **Model B**: V1 social readability for these two is driven by the Phase 15 curse-state overlay (known-werewolf/known-vampire visibility), not an independent Daedric counter; the per-tier price descriptions carry the social-register texture. Removed the 6 independent stigma rows from `race-sheets/PDV_DaedricContent_Manifest.md` (now 403 Daedric rows), replaced with curse-state-driven notes, and updated the Coverage table. The pulled lines plus a properly-designed enhancement are saved in `references/authoring/PDV_V2_Backlog.md` Section 3: a **witness-based** notoriety signal (accrues when caught killing as a werewolf / feeding as a vampire), bands `Suspected/Known/Notorious`, and at `Known`+ the **Vigilants of Stendarr** hunt and randomly attack. Also applied a wording fix: Hircine Seeker price now reads "The civilized world **senses** the beast in you and keeps its distance." Content verifier clean.

- **Khajiit reclassified Legible (not Native) for Mephala/Boethiah + Daedric review fixes (2026-06-01):** Daedric prose review found a routing gap: the global Mephala/Boethiah paths routed Khajiit out to a "native" treatment that did not exist (Khajiit implements five lunar focuses -- Khenarthi/Azurah/Baan Dar/Rajhin/Alkosh -- with no Mafala/Boethra). Azura is genuinely native via Azurah; Mafala/Boethra are marginal Khajiiti names, not lunar lanes. Reclassified Khajiit `Native -> Legible` for Mephala and Boethiah in `references/phase4/PDV_DaedricRacePrinceMatrix.csv`, authored two global responses (`PDV_Msg_Daedric_{Boethiah,Mephala}_Response_Khajiit`, mirroring the Hermorah/Sangiin "legible through the native name, but not a lunar lane" pattern), and updated the Daedric manifest native-integration notes (Section 2, Boethiah 6.8, Mephala 7.2, coverage table). Daedric rows 403->405. Also: Boethiah neglect line trimmed off the 80/80 cap; Azura Nord response title "A Foreign Star" -> "A Taboo Star" (matrix state is Taboo); Hermaeus Mora Khajiit response "Lunar Mandate" -> "the Lattice" (canonical term); Peryite verified clean. Content verifier clean (FAIL=0, WARN=0); matrix fidelity holds.

- **Pre-beta race testing closeout and shrine-prayer alias rule (2026-06-29 AEST):** Breton Hidden Art, Orc organic life-mode, and Nord current beta-feel packets are evidence-recorded in `PDV_Phase20_ManualEvidenceLedger.json`; at that closeout the strict beta audit failed only on Imperial and Dunmer race manual/runtime evidence plus the claim boundary. Superseded queue note: Imperial passed on 2026-07-04, leaving Dunmer as the remaining race blocker. Nord final testing produced several live fixes: Book of Days and the focused Prisma panel are player-owned only, dashboard piety drivers are recorded for every nonzero deity movement, broad Old Ways T1 syncs immediately, overlapping legacy Aedric boons for manager-owned race reward families are opted out/cleared, Talos/Kyne public labels and Book of Days race labels are normalized, and Talos betrayal/neglect/commitment surfaces are compile/readback clean with runtime tester proof. Vanilla shrine activation now suppresses vanilla shrine gameplay effects and feeds a hidden once-per-day PDV shrine-prayer signal where configured; backend piety may award multiple cultural aliases from one shrine, but Book of Days writes exactly one player-origin-appropriate deity name (for example Nord Kynareth shrine prayer writes Kyne, Khajiit writes Khenarthi). Nocturnal and Dragonborn Good Daedra shrines remain outside the generic +2 Aedric shrine-prayer bridge to avoid double-award design drift. Closeout gates: `pdv_compile` for `PDV_ShrinePrayerEffect`, `PDV_EventBus`, `PDV__ManagerQuest`, and `PDV_MCM`; `pdv-shrine-blessing-author --check`; `pdv_prisma_ui_audit`; `pdv_legacy_boon_overlap_audit`; `pdv_verify --json` with FAIL=0 and only the existing medallion glyph fallback warning; `pdv_beta_readiness_audit --strict --json` remained NOT_BETA_READY at the time due to Imperial and Dunmer.

### Archived decisions

Older entries are archived in `archive/PDV_DecisionsLog_Archive_2026-05.md`,
which carries a full dated index. Archived coverage: foundational
architecture/toolchain decisions, closed Phase 1-9 proofs, V3
preflight/slices/Phase 7 closeout, the race-architecture implementation-lock
pass, Daedric and contextual-favor design locks, Prisma UI scaffolding, lessons
intake, and the 2026-05-11 -> 2026-05-16 session notes. Live invariants from
those entries are restated under Architecture Summary / Naming Conventions;
active process rules under Standing Rules above.

**Rolling-window convention:** keep the current phase arc here in full. At
doc-sync time, roll entries for fully-closed phases that predate the active arc
into the dated archive (leaving the title+date in the archive index), and
promote any still-active rule into Standing Rules rather than archiving it
silently. Start a new dated archive file per month when the current one grows
large.
